Let's analytically consider support design. The sum indices i+j. We can choose support B. For a given B and weights w, coefficient c_s = Σ_{i<j, i+j=s} 2w_iw_j + Σ_{i=i, 2i=s} w_i^2. This is sum over edges in a graph G where vertices B, edges correspond to sum s with color s; each s bucket contains disjoint edges (a matching? For fixed s, pairs {i,j} with i+j=s form at most one edge per i; map i -> s-i; since distinct, edges disjoint; size ≤m/2). c_s is sum of products over a matching. We want all matching sums small. This resembles assigning vertex weights to control weighted matchings.

If B is an "almost" Sidon set with few collisions, we can assign weights recursively placed to keep pair products bounded. For large m, complete graph colorings by sums: each edge colored by sum. The max coefficient is max over color of sum of endpoint weight products (diagonal separate). We can choose B to minimize max color degree/structure.

Let's design a fast smooth solver:
- Input a normalized sum=1, length n.
- For iteration:
   - c = conv(a,a) (using np.convolve direct or FFT).
   - Compute max_c. Φ_actual = 2n*max_c.
   - Compute weights w_k. Options:
        * Softmax: z = c / T; w = exp(z - max(z))/sum. T can be max_c/β? For β as number of active. If β=1, w emphasizes max (approximately). We can maintain β annealing from small (e.g. 0.05 of max? ) to large (e.g. 1000) to focus.
     To avoid underflow, w=exp((c-max)/T). If T small, only max weights.
        * Power: w=(c^p)/(sum c^p), p adaptive. For zeros, okay. p=1 gives uniform; p=10 focuses.
   - Compute gradient g = 2 * crosscorr(a, w) (with w length 2n-1). If w normalized sum=1, gradient direction same as weighted average of active gradients. Need confirm normalization doesn't matter. For logsumexp with w normalized, gradient =2 crosscorr. Good.
   - Update a_new = project_simplex(a - step*g). Could use FISTA: y = a - step*g? FISTA uses y momentum. For smooth (nonconvex) maybe use restart if objective increases.
   - Evaluate actual score. Keep best.

Potential risk: Projected gradient with small steps may get stuck. Use multiple time splits:
- Phase A: coarse smoothing with small β (w broad) to move a away from initial bad shapes, step large (e.g. 0.5) and many iterations until score stable.
- Phase B: anneal β larger to focus on max, decrease step.
- Periodic random perturbations and restarts from best.

What about using discrete optimization directly. We can build a highly optimized solver with multiple strategies:

Projected gradient with backtracking and full soft weights:
Pseudo:
```
def crosscorr_grad(a, w, N):
   # w length L=2n-1
   N = next power of 2 >= len(a)+len(w)-1
   A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   W = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), N) # zero pad
   c = np.fft.irfft(A*W, N)[:len(a)+len(w)-1]
   L=len(w); n=len(a)
   return 2.0 * c[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]  # careful slicing
```

For actual conv score, we can direct? Use `irfft(A*A)` real; small negative due FFT set to 0? fine. At final verify with evaluate_sequence direct.

Let's write efficient functions:
```
def next_pow2(n): return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()

def conv_fft(x, N=None):
   n=len(x)
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   X=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
   y=np.fft.irfft(X*X,N)
   return y[:2*n-1]

def conv_grad(r, a, N3):
   # r/w length 2n-1, a length n
   L=2*n-1
   W=np.fft.rfft(np.fliplr? `np.flip(w)`, N3)
   C=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N3)[:n+L-1]
   seg = C[L-1:L-n-1:-1]? Need slice excluding end 0? We need t from L-1 down to L-n inclusive. Python slice [L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
   return seg  # length n
```
Then r_dir = 0.5*grad_c? Since grad_c=2*seg, sum=2. Use seg normalized sum already 1 if w sum1 and a sum1? Check r=seg=∑ w a; sum r =∑ w ∑a =1. Good. So r is exactly normalized gradient direction. Great.

   v = a - alpha * r

Instead of projecting on simplex after subtract, we can perform a proportional multiplicative update (mirror descent) that naturally positive: a_new_i ∝ a_i * exp(-η r_i), normalize. This is nice: moves mass inversely to gradient, preserves positivity, never zero. Since r_i sum1. For small η, proportional changes. This is FISTA in exponential family. It might be more stable and no projection complexity. Let's analyze.

Multiplicative update: a_new = normalize( a * exp(-η r) ). Since r sum1. If r has high values at indices that contribute to high conv, reduce them. This is like natural gradient / mirror descent. It can be very effective. Objective nonconvex but heuristic.

Parameterization z (log a) update: z_new = z - η r - mean? This is similar to Adam but fixed lr. In logit, precomputed r is gradient of real coordinate. Natural gradient step in exponential family: a_new ∝ a exp(-η r). We can implement with log: z += -η r - mean. This directly updates logits without computing gradient wrt z; simpler and more stable. It is exactly gradient descent on entropy-regularized? This may be better than projected Euclidean.

We can use two updates: multiplicative (log) and Euclidean (simplex). Try both.

Multiplicative update with r normalized sum1. η scales relative changes. If r uniform 1/n, exp(-η/n) nearly uniform so a unchanged (plus normalization) — good. If r concentrated (some 0.1), those coordinates reduce by exp(-0.1η). η=1 reduces by 10%; η=5 by 60%. Need line search on η. Candidate score. This is scale-invariant. Could be very effective. Let's design:
```
def eval_score(a, n): ...
def mul_update(a, r, eta):
   # a positive, sum1
   loga = np.full? a may have zeros; use eps.
   logs = np.log(a + eps) - eta * r
   logs -= logs.max()
   b=exp(logs); b/=b.sum()
   return b
```
Since a from softmax positive, no zeros. We can maintain z directly: z_new = z - eta*r; z_new -= z_new.mean(); a=exp(z - max)/sum. This avoids log(a). Starting z from initial a. r computed from a.

This is non-Euclidean projected gradient with at entropy. It may handle sparsity if η large (coords near zero). It might converge. We can line search η: try [0.1,1.0,5.0,20.0]? But η too large may zero out and get stuck. Use adaptive: attempt base η, if score improves increase up to 100; if fragments, decrease. Or maintain best.

Can also mix Euclidean: a_new = (1-λ)a + λ mul? Or project simplex of a-αr.

Let's think about objective under multiplicative update. For a unique active lag central, r_i ≈ a_{n-1-i}; update reduces positions paired with high partner. This is like repulsion. It may produce alternating pattern.

Could combine with random initialization and simulated annealing: η schedule high→low.

How to choose weights r? If use p-norm with p high, r corresponds to reducing high coefficients. But multiplicative update with high p may chase local max. Good.

Let's draft optimization routine using log/multiplicative:
```
def optimize_log(a0, n, time_budget, rng, initial_power=2, final_power=200):
   z = np.log(a0 + 1e-15)
   z -= z.mean()
   best_a=a0 copy; best_score=fast_score
   eta=1.0
   power=initial_power
   no_improve=0
   while time:
      a = softmax(z,n)
      c = conv_fft(a)
      m = c.max()
      if m <= 0: break
      score = 2*n*m
      if score<best: update
      # update power based on iterations: power *=1.005? or min(500, power*1.01)
      w = (c/m)**power
      s=w.sum()
      if s<1: w uniform? ; w/=s
      # compute r = crosscorr(a,w) segment
      r = conv_grad(a,w)
      # r should sum≈1

Detailed parametric random init:
```
def trigonometric_init(n, rng, order=10, amp=0.8):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    z = np.zeros(n)
    for r in range(order):
        # random freq between 1 and 5 cycles? Since sum zero if integer freq; use integer to ensure mean zero.
        f = rng.integers(1, 10)
        theta = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        amp_r = amp * rng.uniform(0.1,1.0) / np.sqrt(r)
        z += amp_r * (np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x)+theta*np.sin(...))? 
    # ensure z mean zero
    z -= z.mean()
    a = exp(z); a/=sum
```
Integer frequencies ensure sum nearly zero, but exp positive. This can produce oscillations. For powers, clip z to [-2,2] to not too sparse. Many random starts; optimize best.

def fast_score_conv(a, N=None, return_c=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if N is None:
        N = next_power_two(3*n - 2)  # large enough; for score only 2n-1 but okay
    X = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(X*X, N)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    c[max(c) < 0?]...
    mn = float(np.max(c))
    if mn < 0: mn=0.0
    sc = 2.0 * n * mn  # sum is 1 if a normalized; if not normalize? We'll always sum1. If not, score = 2n*max/(sum^2). For safety:
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <=0: return inf
    sc = 2.0*n*mn/(s*s)
    if return_c: return sc, c
    return sc
```

def softmax_z(z):
    zmax = z.max()
    expz = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = expz.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: fall back uniform
    return expz/s
```

def optimize_sequence(a0, n, duration, rng, p_start=2.0, p_end=80.0, eta0=0.3, verbose=False):
    N = next_power_two(3*n - 2)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+duration
    a = a0.copy(); normalize
    z = z_from_a(a)
    c = conv_fft(a,N); best_score, score = ...
    best_a = a.copy()
    eta=eta0; p=p_start
    restart_count=0; no_improve=0; total_iter=0
    current_c = c
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,N) # store? For current conv we have A? conv_fft didn't return; recompute for performance? We'll compute A in iteration.
    while time < deadline:
       if no_improve > 3000 or ...: restart
       # update p based on time/iters
       p = min(p_end, p_start + total_iter*0.005)
       # maybe randomize p if restart: p = rng.uniform(2,32)
       # weights
       w = soft_weights(current_c, p)
       # r
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       r = weighted_shift_with_A(a,w,N,A)
       if not finite or sum<=0: break
       # candidate list
       cands=[]
       # log eta
       for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*2]:
          zc, ac = make_log_candidate(z,r,e)
          # if too similar maybe skip? 
          sc, cc = eval(ac,N,n)
          cands.append((sc, ac, cc, None, e))
       # euclid alpha
       alpha = 0.05 if eta<1 else 0.1
       ae = make_euclid_candidate(a,r,alpha)
       sce, ce = eval(ae,N,n)
       cands.append((sce, ae, ce, z_from_a(ae), 0))
       # choose best sc
       best_cand = min(cands, key=lambda x: x[0]) # cannot use lambda! Use loop.
```
No lambda. We'll loop.

`soft_weights`:
```
def soft_weights(c, p):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    nC = len(c)
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
       return np.full(nC, 1.0/nC)
    t = c / m
    # t might tiny negative
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    if p <= 1.0:
       w = t.copy()
    else:
       # for speed, if p integer? float
       w = np.power(t, p)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w = np.full(nC, 1.0/nC)
    else:
       w /= s
       # add floor
       floor = 1e-5 / nC
       w = w * (1.0 - floor*nC?) Actually floor sum=1e-5.
       w = (1.0 - 1e-5) * w + floor
       w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
If c values all under max zero? okay.

`weighted_shift_with_A` must handle `w` sum1. Use `np.flip(w)`; a large array each iter. Could preallocate? Not necessary. But `np.flip` creates view? It returns view with negative stride; rfft can handle? Might need contiguous; rfft handles. Could use `w[::-1]`.

`r = cseg`; normalize:

r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
rs = float(np.sum(r))
if rs <= 1e-300: r = np.full(n,1/n)
else: r /= rs
```

Project_simplex implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not cond.any(): return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    k = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[k] - 1.0) / (k+1.0)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    return w
```
This is standard. For v maybe large negative, okay.

Candidate Eucl: if `alpha` very large, v sum=1-alpha, projection may uniformish. OK.

Learning/log clipping:
```
def adjust_z(z, clip=50.0):
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    zmin = z.min(); zmax=z.max(); span = zmax-zmin
    if span > 2*clip:
       # rescale around mean to span 2*clip-1
       z = (z - np.mean(z)) * ((2*clip - 1.0)/span)
    return np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
```
If span huge, scale; probabilities change but prevents overflow.

`z_after_log(z,r,eta)`:
```
zc = z - eta * r
return adjust_z(zc)
```
Since r sum1, mean r=1/n. zc mean = zmean - eta/n; adjust subtract new mean. OK.

Candidate generation with log zc. We need also score. `score_conv(a,N)` returns c; if return c. To avoid allocating c for all candidates? We need c for accepted. For rejected, can discard. Implement:
```
def score_and_conv(a,N,n):
    X=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(X*X,N)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(np.max(c)); if m<0: m=0
    return 2.0*n*m, c

Interpolation function:
```
def resample_sequence(arr, new_n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr,float); arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
    s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: return full
    arr/=s
    old_n=len(arr)
    if old_n==new_n: return arr
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
    y=np.maximum(y,0)
    sy=y.sum(); if sy>0: y/=sy
    return y
```
This linearly interpolates order. If arr has spikes, may smooth. OK.

def trigonometric_init(n, rng, order=12, amp=1.5):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    z = np.zeros(n)
    for r in range(order):
        f = rng.integers(1, max(2, n//4))? Need integer frequency to keep sum zero? For n large, freq 1..10.
        phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
        amp_r = amp * np.exp(-0.3*r) * rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)
        z += amp_r * np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x/n + phase)  # if frequency integer? f ensures periodic? cos(2π f k/n)
    z -= z.mean()
    z = np.clip(z, -zmax, zmax)
    a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum(); return a
```

def geometric_init(n, tau, side=0):

   normalize

def adapt_weights(c, p, M_target):
    m = c.max()
    if m <= 0: uniform
    # base active weights
    t = np.maximum(c/m,0)
    w_base = np.power(t,p)
    s = w_base.sum()
    if s <=0: uniform; else w_base/=s
    # target violation weights (quadratic excess)
    if M_target > 0 and m > M_target:
        exc = np.maximum(c - 0.98*M_target, 0.0)
        s2=exc.sum()
        if s2>0:
           w_target = exc / s2
           # mix: if m far above target, more target?
           ratio = m / M_target
           mix = 0.0
           if ratio > 1.1: mix=0.5
           elif ratio > 1.05: mix=0.3
           elif ratio > 1.0: mix=0.1
           # Actually if ratio high, target weights still only on top above target, maybe good. Use mix=min(0.8, 0.2*ratio)
           w = (1-mix)*w_base + mix*w_target; w/=w.sum()
        else: w=w_base
    else: w=w_base
    floor ...
```

def make_weights(c,p,Mtarget):
   m=max; t=c/m; wb=power(t,p)/sum
   if m > Mtarget:
       exc = c - Mtarget; exc=np.maximum(exc,0); s=exc.sum()
       if s>0:
          wt=exc/s
          # increase mix as m/Mtarget-1
          mix = 0.3
          r=m/Mtarget
          if r>1.5: mix=0.6
          elif r>2.0: mix=0.8
          w=(1-mix)*wb+mix*wt; w/=w.sum()
       else: w=wb
   else: w=wb
   floor
```

def optimize_sequence(a0, n, duration, rng, target_score=1.50, label=""):
    if duration <=0: return a0, fast...

    a = np.asarray(a0,float); normalize; sum check
    z = z_from_a(a)
    c = conv_fft(a,N); score = score_from_c(c,n)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_score=score; best_z=z.copy()
    eta=0.5; p=2.0
    no_improve=0; total=0; restart=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if no_improve > 1500:
          restart_count...
       if restart >8 and no_improve>... maybe break? But allocated run ends.
       # update p
       p = min(24.0, p + 0.003)
       Mtarget = target_score/(2.0*n)
       w = make_weights(c,p,Mtarget)
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       r = weighted_shift_with_A(a,w,N,A)
       if r is None: break
       # candidate loop
       base_score = 2*n*float(np.max(c))
       found = False
       # We'll evaluate candidates in order of likely: base eta, 0.5 eta, 2 eta, euclid
```

Next power two function:
```
def next_power_two(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def normalize_nonneg(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return None
    a/=s
    return a
```

Let's code `make_weights` carefully:
```
def make_weights(c, p, Mtarget):
    L = len(c)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
       return np.full(L, 1.0/L, dtype=float)
    t = c / m
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    if p <= 1.0:
       wb = t.copy()
    elif p >= 4.0:
       # maybe use exp(p*(log(t))) for t>0 to avoid underflow? np.power ok.
       wb = np.power(t, p)
    else:
       wb = np.power(t, p)
    s = float(np.sum(wb))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       w = np.full(L, 1.0/L, dtype=float)
    else:
       w = wb / s
       if m > Mtarget and Mtarget > 0:
           exc = c - Mtarget
           exc = np.maximum(exc, 0.0)
           s2 = float(np.sum(exc))
           if s2 > 1e-300:
               wt = exc / s2
               ratio = m / Mtarget
               mix = 0.0
               if ratio > 1.0 + 1e-8:
                   mix = 0.05 + 0.5 * (ratio - 1.0)
                   if mix > 0.7: mix = 0.7
               w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * wt
               sw = float(np.sum(w))
               if sw > 0: w /= sw
       # floor
       floor_sum = 1e-5
       w = (1.0 - floor_sum) * w + floor_sum / L
       sw = float(np.sum(w))
       if sw > 0: w /= sw
    return w
```
If p very large and t zeros, `np.power` underflow; sum maybe 1 (max only), okay. If all t underflow except max, w on max. Good.

`weighted_shift_with_A`:
```
def weighted_shift_with_A(a,w,N,A):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    W=np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w[::-1],dtype=float), N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    # linear full length n+L-1
    seg=C[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
    # if seg length not n, pad?
    if len(seg) < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n); tmp[:len(seg)] = seg; seg=tmp
    else:
       seg=seg[:n]
    seg = np.maximum(seg,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(seg))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return None
    seg /= s
    return seg
```
Check slicing for n=1: L=1, L-n-1=-1, `C[0:-1:-1]` empty. We won't run n=1. Could handle: if n==1 return [1].

`conv_fft`:
```
def conv_fft(a,N):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    return C[:2*len(a)-1]
```
We could return A too to reuse. In optimizer, we compute A for gradient; for candidate score no. For current c first, we can compute A once:
```
A = np.fft.rfft(a,N); c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
```
In loop, after accepting log candidate, we need new c; use `conv_fft` returns c and maybe A? We can compute A for next iteration. If accepted candidate is from log with zc, a_cand. We can compute c_cand; next iteration recompute A. Fine.

`score_and_conv`:
```
def score_and_conv(a,N,n):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(np.max(c)); if not finite or m<0: m=0
    sc=2.0*n*m
    return sc,c
```
Again assumes sum1. For candidate Euclid project sum1; log sum1.

If a has tiny negative? project/softmax nonneg. Good.

`adjust_z` with `np.clip` maybe expensive; we can do only if span > 100 to save.
```
def adjust_z(z, clip=50.0):
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    zmax = float(np.max(z)); zmin=float(np.min(z))
    span = zmax - zmin
    if span > 2.0*clip:
        z = (z - float(np.mean(z))) * ((2.0*clip - 1.0) / span)
    return np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
```
If span <= but zmax>clip? Could happen if not centered? We subtract mean, max could >clip even span smaller? If span<=2clip and mean zero, max<=span/2? Not necessarily if all positive? mean zero => if span 60, max 30. If span 90, max 45 <50. So clip enough.

`softmax_z`:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z,float)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    e=np.exp(z - zmax)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0 or not finite: return np.full(len(z),1/len(z))
    return e/s
```

`z_from_a`:
```
def z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,float)
    amin = float(np.min(a))
    if amin <= 0:
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12/min(1, maxamin?))
    z = np.log(a)
    z -= mean
    return adjust_z(z, 40.0)
```
If some a=0, minimum positive maybe. Use `a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)` enough; zeros become 1e-12. But if many small, log huge negative; adjust clip.

In optimizer, initial z from a0 (positive maybe zeros -> 1e-12). Good.

`optimize_sequence` code details:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, n, duration, rng, target_score=1.50, label=""):
    if duration <= 5: return best...
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a is None: a=uniform
    N = next_power_two(3*n-2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c = irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
    score = 2n*max(c)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_z=z_from_a(a)
    eta=0.5; p=2.0; no_improve=0; restart_count=0; total=0
    target_M = target_score/(2.0*n)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+duration
    improves=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and restart_count < 12:
       # p update
       p = min(24.0, p + 0.002)
       w = make_weights(c, p, target_M)
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       r = weighted_shift_with_A(a,w,N,A)
       if r is None: break
       base = score
       best_sc=base; best_a_cand=None; best_c_cand=None; best_z_cand=None; chosen_factor=1.0; found=False
       # candidates
       for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
           zc = z - eta*factor*r
           zc = adjust_z(zc)
           ac = softmax_z(zc)
           sc, cc = score_and_conv(ac,N,n)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best_a_cand=ac; best_c_cand=cc; best_z_cand=zc; chosen_factor=factor; found=True
       # euclid if time
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
           alpha = 0.04 + 0.04 * min(float(eta), 1.0)
           ac = project_simplex(a - alpha*r)
           # ensure normalized
           ac = normalize_nonneg(ac)
           if ac is not None:
              sc, cc = score_and_conv(ac,N,n)
              if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 ... chosen_factor=0
       if found:
           a=best_a_cand; c=best_c_cand; z=best_z_cand; score=best_sc
           if score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
           # adapt eta
           if chosen_factor == 2.0: eta = min(5.0, eta*1.15)
           elif chosen_factor == 0.5: eta = max(0.02, eta*0.8)
           else:
              if no_improve ==0: eta = min(5.0, eta*1.02)
              else: eta = min(5.0, eta*1.0)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           eta = max(0.02, eta*0.8)
           # if stuck, restart
           if no_improve > 400 and restart_count < 12:
               restart_count +=1
               noise = 0.05 * (restart_count)
               if restart_count > 6: noise = 0.25
               z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
               z = adjust_z(z)
               a = softmax_z(z)
               A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
               c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
               m = float(np.max(c)); score = 2.0*n*m if m>0 else 0
               # do not change best
               no_improve=0
               p = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 40.0))
               eta = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 2.0))
               print(...)
           elif no_improve > 1500:
               # if too much, break or small perturb
               restart_count +=1
               noise=0.2
               ...
       total +=1
       if total % 500 ==0: print label current score best.
    # final if current better
    if score < best_score: update
    return best_a, best_score, best_z
```

`generate_initials`:
```
def generate_initials(n, rng, given):
   inits=[]
   if given is not None:
      a=resample_sequence(given,n); if a is not None: inits.append(a)
   inits.append(uniform_array(n))
   for tau in (0.5,1.0):
      inits.append(geometric_init(n,tau,0))
   for i in range(2):
      inits.append(trig_init(n, rng, order=12, amp=1.5))
   # maybe center-symmetric antisymmetric: z = sin(pi*(i+0.5)/n)
   z = np.sin(np.pi * (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n)
   a = np.exp(0.8*z); normalize
   inits.append(a)
   return inits
```
Norm after exp. Ensure new_n etc.

`geometric_init`: `x=np.arange(n)/max(n-1,1)`, if side=0 left, else right. `a=np.exp(-tau*x)`. If tau=0 uniform duplicate; okay.

`trig_init`: Use integer frequencies? If f can be >n? Use f from 1 to 8. `z += amp_r*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*k/n + phase)`. If f not integer relative? It is integer, sum of cos over full period not exactly zero if n not multiple, but mean subtracted. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy
--------
The score is scale invariant, so all candidates are normalized to sum 1.
The main local search is a mirror-descent / projected-gradient hybrid on the
simplex.  The objective is the maximum component of the full convolution.
At each step a weighted subgradient is formed by emphasizing large convolution
coefficients, with an additional emphasis on coefficients still above a target
corresponding to score ~1.50.  For the same subgradient we try several
multiplicative (logistic) updates and one Euclidean simplex-projected update,
then accept the candidate with the best actual score.  Periodic restarts from
the current best point, with random logit noise or a new trigonometric
initialization, are used to escape local minima.  Multiple initializations and,
if time remains, an upsampled version of the best sequence are tried.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def tuple_score(t):
    return float(t[0])


def normalize_nonneg(arr):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def uniform_array(n):
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(max(1, n)), dtype=np.float64)


def next_power_two(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return uniform_array(new_n)
    old_n = len(a)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == 1:
        return uniform_array(new_n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    y = normalize_nonneg(y)
    if y is None:
        return uniform_array(new_n)
    return y


def geometric_init(n, tau, side):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
    if side == 0:
        a = np.exp(-float(tau) * x)
    else:
        a = np.exp(-float(tau) * (1.0 - x))
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a if a is not None else uniform_array(n)


def trigonometric_init(n, rng, order=12, amp=1.5):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    k = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(int(order)):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 9))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ar = float(amp) * float(np.exp(-0.28 * r)) * float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0))
        z += ar * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * k / float(n) + phase)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0)
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    return a if a is not None else uniform_array(n)


def generate_initials(n, rng, given=None):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    inits = []
    if given is not None:
        a = resample_sequence(given, n)
        if a is not None:
            inits.append(a)

    inits.append(uniform_array(n))
    inits.append(geometric_init(n, 0.7, 0))
    inits.append(geometric_init(n, 1.4, 0))
    inits.append(geometric_init(n, 1.0, 1))

    for _ in range(2):
        inits.append(trigonometric_init(n, rng, order=12, amp=1.4))

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = 0.8 * np.sin(np.pi * (x + 0.5) / float(n))
    a = normalize_nonneg(np.exp(z))
    if a is not None:
        inits.append(a)

    return inits


def conv_fft(a, N):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    return c[:2 * len(a) - 1]


def score_and_conv(a, N, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 2500:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m, c


def fast_score_arr(a, N=None, n=None):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    if n is None:
        n = len(a)
    if N is None or N is np.True_:
        N = next_power_two(3 * n - 2)
    sc, _ = score_and_conv(a, N, n)
    return sc


def weighted_shift_with_A(a, w, N, A):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    W = np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(w[::-1], dtype=np.float64), N)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)
    if n == 1:
        r = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        r = C[L - 1:L - n - 1:-1]
        if r.size < n:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            tmp[:r.size] = r
            r = tmp
        elif r.size > n:
            r = r[:n]
    r = np.nan_to_num(r, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(r))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return r / s


def make_weights(c, p, Mtarget):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    m = float(np.max(c)) if L else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(max(1, L)), dtype=np.float64)

    t = c / m
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    wb = np.power(t, float(p))
    s = float(np.sum(wb))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = wb / s
        if Mtarget > 0.0 and m > Mtarget:
            exc = c - Mtarget
            exc = np.maximum(exc, 0.0)
            s2 = float(np.sum(exc))
            if s2 > 1e-300:
                wt = exc / s2
                ratio = m / Mtarget
                mix = 0.0
                if ratio > 1.0 + 1e-8:
                    mix = 0.05 + 0.5 * (ratio - 1.0)
                    if mix > 0.70:
                        mix = 0.70
                w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * wt
                sw = float(np.sum(w))
                if sw > 1e-300:
                    w = w / sw
                else:
                    w = wt
        # Tiny floor so no coefficient is completely ignored.
        floor_sum = 1e-5
        w = (1.0 - floor_sum) * w + (floor_sum / float(L))
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if sw > 1e-300:
            w = w / sw
    return w


def adjust_z(z, clip=50.0):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    zmin = float(np.min(z))
    span = zmax - zmin
    if span > 2.0 * clip:
        z = (z - float(np.mean(z))) * ((2.0 * clip - 1.0) / span)
    return np.clip(z, -clip, clip)


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return uniform_array(len(z))
    return e / s


def z_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    z = np.log(a)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return adjust_z(z, 40.0)


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n <= 0:
        return v.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * rho > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[k] - 1.0) / float(k + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def log_restart_from_best(best_z, n, rng, restart_count, label, target_score, N, best_score):
    if restart_count <= 4:
        noise = 0.05 * float(restart_count)
        if restart_count >= 4:
            noise = 0.25
        z = best_z + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
        z = adjust_z(z)
        a = softmax_z(z)
        tag = "noise"
    else:
        a = trigonometric_init(n, rng, order=16, amp=1.8)
        if a is None:
            a = uniform_array(n)
        z = z_from_a(a)
        tag = "trig"
    sc, c = score_and_conv(a, N, n)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = best_score
    print(f"    {label}: restart {restart_count} ({tag}), score={sc:.8f}, best={best_score:.8f}")
    return z, a, c, sc


def optimize_sequence(a0, n, duration, rng, target_score=1.50, label=""):
    """
    Local search from a0 for the given fixed length n.
    Returns (best_array, best_score) under FFT/fast scoring.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    duration = float(duration)
    if duration <= 5.0:
        a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
        if a is None:
            a = uniform_array(n)
        sc, _ = score_and_conv(a, next_power_two(3 * n - 2), n)
        return a, sc

    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    if a is None:
        a = uniform_array(n)

    N = next_power_two(3 * n - 2)
    Mtarget = float(target_score) / (2.0 * float(n))

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * m

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_z = z_from_a(a)

    z = best_z
    eta = 1.0
    p = 2.0
    local_stall = 0
    restart_count = 0
    total = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + duration

    max_restarts = 12

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        total += 1

        p = min(28.0, p + 0.0035)
        w = make_weights(c, p, Mtarget)
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        r = weighted_shift_with_A(a, w, N, A)
        if r is None:
            break

        base_score = score
        found = False
        best_cand_score = base_score
        best_cand_a = None
        best_cand_c = None
        best_cand_z = None
        chosen_factor = 1.0

        # Multiplicative / log-domain candidates.
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.75:
                break
            zc = z - (float(eta) * factor) * r
            zc = adjust_z(zc)
            ac = softmax_z(zc)
            sc, cc = score_and_conv(ac, N, n)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_cand_score - 1e-12:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand_a = ac
                best_cand_c = cc
                best_cand_z = zc
                chosen_factor = factor
                found = True

        # One Euclidean simplex-projected candidate.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.75:
            alpha = 0.05 + 0.05 * min(float(eta) / 2.0, 1.0)
            ac = project_simplex(a - alpha * r)
            ac = normalize_nonneg(ac)
            if ac is not None:
                sc, cc = score_and_conv(ac, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score - 1e-12:
                    best_cand_score = sc
                    best_cand_a = ac
                    best_cand_c = cc
                    best_cand_z = z_from_a(ac)
                    chosen_factor = 0.0
                    found = True

        if found:
            a = best_cand_a
            c = best_cand_c
            z = best_cand_z
            score = best_cand_score

            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                local_stall = 0
                if best_score < 1.505:
                    print(f"    {label}: new best score={best_score:.8f} (iter {total})")
            else:
                local_stall += 1

            if chosen_factor == 2.0:
                eta = min(20.0, eta * 1.18)
            elif chosen_factor == 0.5:
                eta = max(0.03, eta * 0.78)
            elif chosen_factor == 1.0:
                eta = min(20.0, eta * (1.02 if local_stall == 0 else 1.0))
            else:
                eta = min(20.0, eta * 1.05)

        else:
            local_stall += 1
            eta = max(0.03, eta * 0.80)
            p = min(28.0, p + 0.05)

            if local_stall > 900 and restart_count < max_restarts:
                restart_count += 1
                z, a, c, score = log_restart_from_best(
                    best_z, n, rng, restart_count, label, target_score, N, best_score
                )
                local_stall = 0
                eta = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
                p = float(rng.uniform(2.0, 32.0))

            if restart_count >= max_restarts and local_stall > 3000:
                break

        if total % 800 == 0:
            print(f"    {label}: iter={total}, current={score:.8f}, best={best_score:.8f}, eta={eta:.3f}, p={p:.2f}")

    if score < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = score
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point used by the evaluator.  Uses the available time to search
    for a non-negative sequence with minimal evaluate_sequence score.
    """
    budget = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240517)
    start_t = time.monotonic()

    # Use a safety margin for final validation and return.
    safe_budget = max(60.0, budget - 60.0)
    hard_deadline = start_t + safe_budget
    max_n = 10000

    # Read supplied previous state-of-the-art if present.
    given = None
    try:
        given_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)  # noqa: F821
        given = normalize_nonneg(given_arr)
    except Exception:
        given = None

    if given is None or len(given) < 50:
        n0 = 4806
        given = None
    else:
        n0 = len(given)

    n0 = int(min(max_n, n0))
    if n0 < 100:
        n0 = 4806
        given = None

    if given is not None and len(given) != n0:
        given = resample_sequence(given, n0)

    print(f"=== propose_candidate: budget={budget:.1f}s, main n={n0} ===")

    inits = generate_initials(n0, rng, given)
    N0 = next_power_two(3 * n0 - 2)

    init_scores = []
    for a in inits:
        a = normalize_nonneg(a)
        if a is None:
            continue
        sc = fast_score_arr(a, N0, n0)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            init_scores.append((sc, a))

    if not init_scores:
        a = uniform_array(n0)
        init_scores.append((2.0, a))

    init_scores.sort(key=tuple_score)
    global_best_a = init_scores[0][1].copy()
    global_best_score = float(init_scores[0][0])
    print(f"Best initial: score={global_best_score:.8f}")

    # Run several local searches on the main length.
    slots = []
    slots.append((init_scores[0][1], 0.56))
    if len(init_scores) >= 2:
        slots.append((init_scores[1][1], 0.20))
    else:
        slots.append((trigonometric_init(n0, rng, 16, 1.6), 0.20))
    if len(init_scores) >= 3:
        slots.append((init_scores[2][1], 0.15))
    # The remaining approximate 9% is left for upsample or slack.

    finalists = [(global_best_score, global_best_a)]

    for idx, (init_a, frac) in enumerate(slots):
        rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 45.0:
            break
        alloc = min(0.98 * rem, max(20.0, frac * safe_budget))
        label = f"run{n0}_{idx}"
        ba, bs = optimize_sequence(init_a, n0, alloc, rng, target_score=1.50, label=label)
        print(f"{label}: returned score={bs:.8f}, global best={global_best_score:.8f}, best={bs:.8f}")
        if np.isfinite(bs) and bs < global_best_score - 1e-12:
            global_best_score = float(bs)
            global_best_a = ba.copy()
            finalists.append((global_best_score, global_best_a))
        elif np.isfinite(bs) and len(finalists) < 12:
            finalists.append((float(bs), ba.copy()))

    # If there is time, try a finer resampling of the best sequence.
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0:
        n_up = int(min(max_n, max(2 * n0, n0 + 2000)))
        if n_up != n0:
            a_up = resample_sequence(global_best_a, n_up)
            N_up = next_power_two(3 * n_up - 2)
            sc_up = fast_score_arr(a_up, N_up, n_up)
            print(f"Upsample n={n_up}, initial score={sc_up:.8f}")
            if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < global_best_score + 0.02:
                rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 120.0:
                    ba, bs = optimize_sequence(a_up, n_up, max(20.0, 0.10 * safe_budget), rng,
                                               target_score=1.50, label=f"upsample_{n_up}")
                    print(f"upsample: returned score={bs:.8f}, global best={global_best_score:.8f}")
                    if np.isfinite(bs) and bs < global_best_score - 1e-12:
                        global_best_score = float(bs)
                        global_best_a = ba.copy()
                        finalists.append((global_best_score, global_best_a))
                    elif np.isfinite(bs):
                        finalists.append((float(bs), ba.copy()))

    # Final validation with the real evaluator on a small number of candidates.
    finalists.sort(key=tuple_score)
    best_final_list = [float(x) for x in global_best_a]
    best_final_value = np.inf
    seen = set()

    for sc, arr in finalists:
        if len(seen) >= 4:
            break
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            key = (len(arr), float(np.linalg.norm(arr[:min(64, len(arr))]) + 1e-3 * np.linalg.norm(arr[-min(64, len(arr)):][::-1])))
        except Exception:
            key = (len(arr), float(np.sum(arr[:min(64, len(arr))])))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_final_value:
            best_final_value = val
            best_final_list = lst
        print(f"Final candidate evaluated (fast={sc:.8f}, evaluate={val if np.isfinite(val) else 'inf'})")

    if not np.isfinite(best_final_value):
        best_final_list = [float(x) for x in global_best_a]
        try:
            best_final_value = float(evaluate_sequence(best_final_list))  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            best_final_value = float("inf")

    print(f"=== propose_candidate final score: {best_final_value:.8f}, length={len(best_final_list)} ===")
    return best_final_list
```
